# Skills · Q · part 5

862 entries, qa-design-check to qa-issue-api. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **qa-design-check**: Compare a Figma frame against a live page and report every concrete mismatch (colours, fonts, spacing, radii, geometry), plus extra elements, differing copy and repeated elements that disagree with each other. Use when checking a redesign implementation, verifying a mock was built correctly, or when the user pastes a Figma link together with a site URL. Triggers on "design check", "figma vs site", "compare the mock with the site", "check the redesign", "pixel perfect". ([stokrattt/stokratt-plugins](https://github.com/stokrattt/stokratt-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/qa-design-check/skills/qa-design-check) · ★1)
- **qa-design-review**: Designer's eye QA: finds visual inconsistency, spacing issues, hierarchy problems, AI slop patterns, and slow interactions — then fixes them. Iteratively fixes issues in source code, committing each fix atomically and re-verifying with before/after screenshots. For report-only mode, use /plan-design-review instead. ([g4rcez/dotfiles](https://github.com/g4rcez/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.ai/skills/qa-design-review) · ★48 · 4 copies)
- **qa-design-test-cases**: Use when an outsourcing SDLC work item explicitly requires 'Design Test Cases' during test planning, execution, and quality assessment. ([RadteamBaoDA/ragflow-simple-ui](https://github.com/RadteamBaoDA/ragflow-simple-ui/tree/HEAD/sample-code/sample-agents/qa/qa-design-test-cases) · ★1)
- **qa-design-tests**: Design a focused, risk-based set of tests from requirements, code, models, incidents, or change descriptions. Use when creating or reviewing test cases, automated checks, regression coverage, boundary and equivalence tests, state-transition tests, decision tables, combinatorial tests, properties, coverage goals, or mutation-testing ideas. Use after clarifying material ambiguity; do not use for executing an exploratory session. ([Findlee/Mobile-Apps-Production-Skills](https://github.com/Findlee/Mobile-Apps-Production-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-design-tests))
- **qa-designer-agent**: Run Spectra visual QA design reviews end-to-end with `spectra_qa_agent`, from screenshot capture to UI/UX bug triage and minimal code fixes. Use when asked to run `--design-review`, inspect screenshot regressions, update `plans/QA_design_review.md`, or apply targeted theme/ImGui/overlay rendering polish tied to QA findings. ([danlil240/Spectra](https://github.com/danlil240/Spectra/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-designer-agent) · ★3)
- **qa-designer-agent**: Runs Spectra visual QA with spectra_qa_agent --design-review: 57 screenshots, triage P0–P3 in plans/QA_design_review.md, minimal theme/ImGui fixes. Use for UI polish, visual bugs, design review, or screenshot regressions. ([danlil240/Spectra](https://github.com/danlil240/Spectra/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/qa-designer-agent) · ★3)
- **qa-designer-agent**: Multi-skill QA test design agent. Skills available — technique-selection, coverage-estimation, test-case-design, design-update. Turns requirements, exploration outputs, and diagrams into Zephyr-compatible test cases covering web-ui, mobile-ui (iOS/Android), and API. Always selects the right test technique per module, estimates coverage before generating cases, and uses a 14-column flat Zephyr table. Use after requirement-agent and explorer-agent complete. ([swongsuddee/windflu-test](https://github.com/swongsuddee/windflu-test/tree/HEAD/.agents/toolkit/qa-designer-agent) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **qa-desk**: Run this session as a repo's QA desk: owns the main worktree, runs QA briefs from other sessions one at a time with qa-tester, replies with the verdict. ([sjsimpson/dotfiles](https://github.com/sjsimpson/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/plugin/skills/qa-desk) · ★4)
- **qa-desktop**: QA tests for the Tidybyte for Mac desktop app. Tests the native SwiftUI macOS application including onboarding, scanning, action review, execution, undo, natural language commands, settings, report generation, the menu bar, dashboard and visualizations, keyboard shortcuts, notifications, and error-path flows. Uses agent-browser for desktop GUI testing. ([YORDINDIA/tidybyte](https://github.com/YORDINDIA/tidybyte/tree/HEAD/macos/.agents/skills/qa-desktop) · ★21)
- **qa-desktop-driver-ebitengine**: QA desktop driver for Ebitengine (v2.10.5 or later) games, used by the qa plugin's /qa:run and /qa:create-plan: gives the driver manifest path and how to recognise, configure, plan and script an Ebitengine app tested headless in the background. ([mszenfeld/qa-ebitengine](https://github.com/mszenfeld/qa-ebitengine/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-desktop-driver-ebitengine))
- **qa-detect-a11y**: Detects unnamed buttons, missing lang attribute, absent skip-to-content link, and missing/disabled viewport meta. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-a11y))
- **qa-detect-adaptive-state**: Simulates adaptive media states (prefers-color-scheme: dark, prefers-reduced-motion: reduce, forced-colors: active, prefers-contrast: more) within a single cell via browser_evaluate + matchMedia event synthesis. Detects bugs in each adaptive state without spawning separate cells. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-adaptive-state))
- **qa-detect-breakpoint-boundary**: Detects layout breakage at common CSS breakpoint boundaries (767px, 1023px, 1279px) — widths bugs typically hide between standard test viewports. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-breakpoint-boundary))
- **qa-detect-breakpoint-edge**: Detects layout bugs at exact CSS breakpoint boundaries by extracting the page's actual @media values and probing at (boundary - 1px) vs (boundary + 1px). Catches CSS transitions that work at 390px and 1440px but break at 767/768 or 1023/1024. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-breakpoint-edge))
- **qa-detect-console-errors**: Captures and CLASSIFIES runtime errors during load AND interaction: uncaught exceptions (TypeError/ReferenceError/…), unhandled promise rejections, CSP violations, and console.error — scored by first-party vs third-party origin. Runs in both the deterministic runner and the MCP path. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-console-errors))
- **qa-detect-content-patterns**: Layer 1 of grammar/content 2-layer strategy. Deterministic Haiku probe. Catches: typos (60-word dict), pluralization, untranslated i18n keys, HTML entities, markdown literal, mojibake, lorem ipsum, TODO leaks, long sentences (&gt;30 words), high reading level (Flesch-Kincaid &gt;12), generic CTA copy, homophone candidates (flag for L2 judgment), suspicious-word misspelling candidates (flag for L2 dictionary check). Lang-scoped — skips non-English regions via lang attribute. Reads proper-noun whitelist from probe input. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-content-patterns))
- **qa-detect-css-compat**: Scans CSS stylesheets for properties with known cross-browser compatibility gaps: missing vendor prefixes, Safari-specific traps, Firefox layout quirks, and properties without fallbacks ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-css-compat))
- **qa-detect-dark-mode**: Detects whether the site respects prefers-color-scheme. No dark-mode handling = jarring white flash for dark-mode users ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-dark-mode))
- **qa-detect-dropdown-viewport-clip**: Tests dropdown / tooltip / autocomplete menus: open each, verify the popup is not clipped at the right or bottom edge of the viewport. Runs as ONE in-page async probe (no AI hand-driving). ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-dropdown-viewport-clip))
- **qa-detect-fluid-sweep**: Resize-sweeps the page across a range of widths (incl. in-between 600/900/1100 and extremes 320/1920) to catch fluid-layout breaks that only happen BETWEEN standard breakpoints ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-fluid-sweep))
- **qa-detect-forced-colors**: WCAG 1.4.11 — detects elements that rely solely on background color or images for distinction. Fail Windows High Contrast Mode (forced-colors: active) ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-forced-colors))
- **qa-detect-hidden-resources**: Detects desktop-only elements that are CSS-hidden on mobile but still download heavy resources (images, iframes, videos) — wasting mobile bandwidth without improving UX ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-hidden-resources))
- **qa-detect-hover-touch**: Detects :hover CSS rules that aren't wrapped in @media (hover: hover) — on touch devices these styles get 'stuck' after a tap, leaving buttons/links highlighted indefinitely ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-hover-touch))
- **qa-detect-images**: Detects missing alt text, broken images, oversized images, aspect-ratio distortion (stretched), and hero/banner images without object-fit:cover ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-images))
- **qa-detect-layout**: Detects fixed/sticky overflow, narrow content, CTA below fold, dropdown cutoff, content bleed-through, critical-element-hidden, excessive whitespace, sticky-covers-action, table overflow, fixed header obstructing content, element overlap, grid/flex collapse failures, sticky header too tall on mobile ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-layout))
- **qa-detect-loading**: Detects loading spinners stuck 3s after networkidle, blank pages, and 404/error content even when HTTP returned 200 (SPA routing failures) ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-loading))
- **qa-detect-loading-states**: Tests loading-state behavior under simulated slow network. Detects stuck skeletons, loading spinners overlapping content, lazy-load that fails on slow connections, and below-fold content that never loads. Uses an in-page fetch/XHR interceptor for network throttling (works without CDP). Runs as ONE in-page async probe. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-loading-states))
- **qa-detect-mobile-keyboard**: Detects layout that breaks when the on-screen keyboard appears AND inputs with wrong type/inputmode that show the wrong keyboard on mobile (QWERTY instead of numpad, etc.). Runs as ONE in-page async probe (no AI hand-driving). ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-mobile-keyboard))
- **qa-detect-modal-viewport-fit**: Tests open modals / dialogs: verify they fit within the viewport height, action buttons (Submit/Cancel) are reachable, and content scrolls internally rather than the modal overflowing. Runs as ONE in-page async probe (no AI hand-driving). ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-modal-viewport-fit))
- **qa-detect-network-errors**: Captures HTTP 4xx/5xx responses and failed requests during page load ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-network-errors))
- **qa-detect-orientation**: Detects layout breakage in landscape orientation on mobile/tablet — swaps viewport dimensions and scans for overflow, cut-off content, and unusable navigation. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-orientation))
- **qa-detect-orientation-flip**: Tests TRUE orientation rotation via browser_resize (swap width/height). Detects layout bugs that appear in landscape, state loss across rotation, and missing orientationchange event handling. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-orientation-flip))
- **qa-detect-overflow**: Detects horizontal content overflow on any element ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-overflow))
- **qa-detect-overflow-controls**: Detects tab strips, segmented controls, and button groups whose children overflow horizontally without a visible scroll affordance ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-overflow-controls))
- **qa-detect-reduced-motion**: WCAG 2.3.3 — detects animations/transitions that are not gated by @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce). Vestibular-disorder users get sick from motion. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-reduced-motion))
- **qa-detect-reflow**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/qa-detect-reflow) · ★745)
- **qa-detect-reflow**: WCAG 1.4.10 — detects content that requires horizontal scroll at 320px, fixed-width elements, non-shrinking tables, and oversized images. Runs as ONE in-page async probe (no AI hand-driving). ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-reflow))
- **qa-detect-responsive-images**: Detects images served without srcset, missing max-width:100%, missing loading='lazy' below the fold, and oversized natural dimensions for the viewport ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-responsive-images))
- **qa-detect-rtl-layout**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/qa-detect-rtl-layout) · ★745)
- **qa-detect-rtl-layout**: Switches the document to RTL (dir='rtl') and detects layout breakage — overflow, mirrored asymmetry, and non-logical CSS properties that don't flip. Runs as ONE in-page async probe (no AI hand-driving). ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-rtl-layout))
- **qa-detect-safe-area**: Detects fixed/sticky elements at the top or bottom of the viewport that don't use env(safe-area-inset-*) — content gets hidden behind iPhone notch / home indicator ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-safe-area))
- **qa-detect-stack**: Detect the framework, locales, sections, Figma key, and git context of a JS/TS project for QA tooling profile generation. ([dongquoctien/qa-annotator-extension](https://github.com/dongquoctien/qa-annotator-extension/tree/HEAD/plugins/qa-tooling/skills/qa-detect-stack))
- **qa-detect-sticky-scroll**: Tests sticky elements during scroll: they remain pinned, do not overlap content, and do not detach. Scrolls the page, captures state, restores. Runs as ONE in-page async probe (no AI hand-driving). ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-sticky-scroll))
- **qa-detect-touch**: Detects tap targets that are too small for accurate touch input, using WCAG 2.2 spacing-aware rules: &lt;24×24 always flagged, 24-43 px flagged only when crowded by another clickable target within 24px. Also flags interactive elements &lt;8px apart. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-touch))
- **qa-detect-touch-interactions**: Dispatches real TouchEvent (touchstart/touchend) on elements that have hover-only handlers, to detect controls that work with mouse but not with touch. Tests tap, double-tap-to-zoom suppression, swipe gestures. Runs as ONE in-page async probe. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-touch-interactions))
- **qa-detect-typography**: Detects small fonts, tight line-height, clipped text, oversized headings on mobile, and unbreakable long words causing overflow ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-typography))
- **qa-detect-typography-advanced**: Real typography DEFECTS only (trimmed 2026-06-09 to remove cosmetic/opinion noise): failed web fonts, real WCAG contrast failures, tofu/replacement glyphs, mixed-content (blocked) fonts, and missing/invalid html lang. The ~37 best-practice nitpicks (font-display, preload, baseline-grid, rhythm, payload, widow/measure, px-sizing, etc.) were removed because they are advisories, not bugs. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-typography-advanced))
- **qa-detect-typography-responsive**: Closes the responsive/legibility typography gaps not covered by qa-detect-typography(-advanced): font appropriateness per viewport, fluid (clamp/vw) type scaling, justified-without-hyphens rivers, text over background images without a scrim, px sizing that ignores user font-size preference, and missing tabular figures in numeric tables. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-typography-responsive))
- **qa-detect-ux-actions**: Catches action-button UX problems: destructive actions without confirmation pattern, multiple competing primary CTAs (broken visual hierarchy), disabled buttons without explanation, primary button hidden below the fold. Goes beyond a11y — catches UX intent failures. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-actions))
- **qa-detect-ux-active-state**: Detects inconsistent active-state styling within the same navigation, tab group, or list — when one active item uses a background highlight and another uses only text color, users get confused about what's selected. Catches the 'two different active styles in the same menu' bug class. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-active-state))
- **qa-detect-ux-affordance**: Detects affordance UX failures: clickable elements that don't look clickable, hover-only revealed actions (dead on mobile/touch), missing focus indicators, cursor:default on interactives, text inputs without visible borders. Catches the 'I didn't know I could click that' bug class. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-affordance))
- **qa-detect-ux-alignment**: Detects vertical baseline / inline alignment issues: breadcrumb items at different baselines, label not aligned with its input, icon not aligned with adjacent text, sibling buttons in a row with different heights, table cells in same row with mismatched vertical centers, form action group not aligned to the same side. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-alignment))
- **qa-detect-ux-breadcrumb**: Detects breadcrumb UX problems: parent name same as current page (Students &gt; Students), missing home/root link, only one item (not a breadcrumb), separator missing/inconsistent, current page is a link (should be plain text), no aria-label for nav landmark. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-breadcrumb))
- **qa-detect-ux-card-consistency**: Detects inconsistencies BETWEEN cards on the same page: mixed border-radius values, some cards with shadow and some without, different border colors, different background tones, mismatched widths in same row, mismatched heights with similar content. Catches the 'these cards look like they're from different design systems' bug class. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-card-consistency))
- **qa-detect-ux-card-usage**: Detects illogical use of the card pattern: a giant card containing only a single small input (your Search-only card), card with &gt; 70% empty space relative to content, cards nested 3+ levels deep, card with substantial content but no heading, card heading separated from content by big gap. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-card-usage))
- **qa-detect-ux-chart-render**: Detects broken chart rendering — chart container has scaffolding (axes, gridlines, legend) but no actual data marks (no bars, lines, points); orphan tooltips floating with no data point beneath them; chart canvas mostly empty pixels (&gt;90% background). Catches the 'chart loaded but shows nothing' bug class (your Fees & Expenses screenshot). ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-chart-render))
- **qa-detect-ux-cross-page-theme**: Detects color/theme drift ACROSS pages — sidebar background color changes between routes, header bar uses different hues on different pages, theme primary varies across the audit run, active-state color drifts. The per-cell probe records color fingerprints; scripts/check-cross-page-theme.cjs aggregates them after the run and emits findings. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-cross-page-theme))
- **qa-detect-ux-empty-state-conflict**: Detects the 'system says no results but shows old results anyway' bug class — empty-state toast/banner saying 'Record Not Found' / 'No data' visible at the same time as result cards, rows, or list items still rendered; pagination active during empty state; the same empty-state toast rendered multiple times stacked. Catches your Students filter screenshot bug where 'Record Not Found' toast appeared but the previous RIDA NASIR card stayed visible. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-empty-state-conflict))
- **qa-detect-ux-feedback**: Detects UX feedback gaps: empty states without illustration/CTA, tables with no rows + no empty message, modals without visible close button, modals not dismissible via ESC/backdrop, search bars without clear button. Goes beyond a11y — catches missing user-feedback patterns. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-feedback))
- **qa-detect-ux-format-consistency**: Detects mixed display formats for the same kind of data on a single page: date formats (5/6/2026 vs 2026-06-05 vs Jun 5 2026), currency formats (PKR 93M vs Rs. 93,000,000 vs $93M), number formats (1,000 vs 1.000 vs 1k), time formats (17:17 vs 5:17 PM), phone formats. Catches the 'why is the same data shown three different ways' bug class. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-format-consistency))
- **qa-detect-ux-hover**: Detects hover-state quality issues: buttons/links with no :hover style at all, hover state identical to default state (no visual change), hover color off the site theme, hover transitions missing (abrupt change), icon-only buttons with no hover feedback. Catches the 'button doesn't have good hover' bug class. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-hover))
- **qa-detect-ux-icons**: Catches icon UX problems: icon-only buttons without tooltip, icons without accessible labels, ambiguous icon meaning, oversized/undersized icons, icon-text gap inconsistency. Goes beyond qa-detect-a11y (which only checks aria-label presence) — checks discoverability + UX clarity. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-icons))
- **qa-detect-ux-input-width**: Detects input/select/textarea fields that are too narrow relative to available container width: small input in a wide container, sibling fields with mismatched widths, textarea with same height as a single-line input. Catches the 'why are my inputs so tiny when there's space' bug class. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-input-width))
- **qa-detect-ux-media-shape**: Detects inconsistencies in image/avatar/media visuals on the same page: avatars mixing circle / square / rounded shapes, images in same grid with different aspect ratios (16:9 + 4:3 + square), some images bordered/shadowed and others not, logo rendered at different sizes across the page. ([Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent](https://github.com/Luqman-Ud-Din/blackbox-qa-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/qa-detect-ux-media-shape))
- **qa-detect-ux-modal-form**: Detects modal + form UX issues: modal title singular/plural mismatch ('Update Districts' when editing 1 row), modal not horizontally centered, required-asterisk shown on only some required fields, static info text placed next to inputs looks like another field, action buttons not in standard footer position. - **qa-detect-ux-overlap**: Detects visible elements that physically overlap each other on screen — toolbar buttons sitting on top of table headers, calendar text collision, modal partially behind another modal, sticky banner covering content. - **qa-detect-ux-page-header**: Detects page-header layout problems — page title smaller than metric/content text (broken typographic hierarchy), page title sitting before the home icon in the breadcrumb row, page title text duplicated as a breadcrumb item, home icon not the first breadcrumb item, page title using same style as breadcrumb. - **qa-detect-ux-required-state**: Detects ambiguous required-field state: required field with red border on load (looks like error), required indicated only by color with no text or programmatic marker (WCAG 1.4.1), required-only-marked-by-asterisk (no programmatic indicator), error class without message text, asterisk without legend.
